<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿越虫洞在广义相对论中需要奇异物质（违反能量条件）来维持，这限制了其物理可行性。作者探索在f(T,τ)修正引力理论中，通过物质-扭转耦合，是否能在不引入奇异物理物质的情况下支撑可穿越虫洞解。

Method: 在f(T,τ)=T+βτ线性模型中，采用各向异性流体和平均压力物质拉格朗日量L_m=P_mean=(p_r+2p_t)/3，对Morris-Thorne几何不加预先固定红移函数或形状函数地推导场方程。分析常数红移函数下能量条件成立的充要条件，并系统性检验渐近平坦性、喉部几何约束等。通过分离物理物质与有效源来考察迹耦合的作用，最后分析非恒定红移函数的边缘情形。

Result: 发现当β>8π且b(r)>0时，rb(r)非增是所有能量条件（零、弱、强）成立的充要条件，该条件同时自动满足渐近平坦性和喉部几何要求。代表性解族b(r)=r₀(r₀/r)ⁿ中能量条件的成立范围被精确刻画（n≥1保证零/弱/强能量条件，主能量条件有更严格的下界）。有效源虽违反零能量条件，但物理物质保持非奇异（非奇异物质即可穿越虫洞）。非恒定红移函数虽能改善喉部切向零能量条件，但无法在渐近平坦外部全程维持。

Conclusion: f(T,τ)引力中物质-扭转耦合能够支撑一大类可穿越虫洞解，无需奇异物理物质。这为在修正引力框架下实现可穿越虫洞提供了理论依据，但非恒定红移情形下的改进有限，说明常数红移函数是获得全局满足能量条件的最自然选择。

Abstract: We study static and spherically symmetric traversable wormholes in $f(T,τ)$ gravity, where the torsion scalar $T$ is coupled to the trace $τ$ of the matter energy--momentum tensor. We consider the linear model $f(T,τ)=T+βτ$ with an anisotropic fluid and the mean-pressure matter Lagrangian $\Lm=\Pmean=(p_r+2p_t)/3$. The field equations are obtained for the Morris--Thorne geometry without fixing the redshift or shape function at the outset. For a constant redshift function, the energy-condition problem takes a simple form. On the branch $β>8π$ and for $b(r)>0$, the energy density together with the null, weak, and strong energy conditions is satisfied throughout the spacetime if and only if $r b(r)$ is non-increasing. The same condition also implies asymptotic flatness, $b(r)<r$ outside the throat, and $b'(r_0)\leq -1$. The allowed geometries can therefore be written as $b(r)=r_0^2 h(r)/r$, where $h(r_0)=1$ and $h(r)$ is positive and non-increasing. For the representative family $b(r)=r_0(r_0/r)^n$, the null, weak, and strong energy conditions hold for $n\geq1$, while the dominant energy condition requires $n\geq3(β-2π)/(β-6π)$. We also separate the physical matter from the effective source and show how the trace coupling allows the physical matter to remain non-exotic although the effective source violates the null energy condition. Finally, we examine the marginal case $b(r)=r_0^2/r$ with a non-constant redshift function. A decreasing redshift function can improve the tangential null energy condition at the throat, but this improvement cannot be maintained throughout an asymptotically flat exterior. These results show that the matter--torsion coupling can support a broad class of traversable wormholes without requiring exotic physical matter.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 非线性电动力学、dyonic电荷、五重态和AdS压力的联合效应显著重塑了黑洞的宏观相结构和有效微观相互作用，拓展了对复杂AdS黑洞热力学的理解，并为后续研究提供理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic properties, Joule-Thomson expansion, and microscopic structure of a dyonic Reissner-Nordström-AdS black hole in nonlinear electrodynamics surrounded by quintessence. Within the extended phase-space formalism, analytical expressions for the mass, Hawking temperature, equation of state, heat capacity, Gibbs free energy, Joule-Thomson coefficient, and Ruppeiner curvature are derived. The effects of the nonlinear-electrodynamics parameter $ω$, the quintessence parameters $(c,ε)$, the dyonic charge $Q$, and the pressure $P$ are systematically investigated. The temperature profiles and equation of state exhibit a Van der Waals--like phase structure, with critical points strongly modified by the quintessence and nonlinear electrodynamivs contributions. The heat capacity and Gibbs free energy further reveal the corresponding thermodynamic stability and phase-transition behaviour. The Joule-Thomson analysis shows distinct cooling and heating regions separated by inversion points. In particular, quintessence generally lowers the critical and inversion temperatures, while increasing the charge and varying the nonlinear parameter significantly deform the inversion structure. The Ruppeiner curvature exhibits pronounced peaks and sign changes near critical regions, indicating strong thermodynamic correlations and alternating attractive and repulsive effective microscopic interactions. The limiting cases $ω\sim 1$, $c\sim 0$, and $Q\sim 0$ consistently recover the corresponding Maxwell, no-quintessence, and neutral black hole behaviours. These results demonstrate that the combined effects of nonlinear electrodynamics, dyonic charge, quintessence, and AdS pressure substantially reshape both the macroscopic phase structure and the effective microscopic interactions of the black hole.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了SO(1,3)规范联络在一次Palatini引力构造非保守Herglotz拉格朗日场论中的作用，发现联络的形状与尺度分解构成一个光滑参数空间，从而存在一个"摩擦场论模空间"，其中每个点对应一种不同的形状/尺度联络动力学划分，而所有点的在壳动力学均再现一次Palatini引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已表明一次Palatini引力可被表述为非保守Herglotz拉格朗日场论，但该框架排除了共形模。本文旨在深入分析SO(1,3)规范联络在此行动依赖理论构建中的角色，理解连接分解自由度如何影响理论的构造。

Method: 将SO(1,3)规范联络分解为"形状"和"尺度"两个部分，其中形状部分保留在Herglotz理论中，尺度部分被纳入行动依赖扇区。通过证明连接分解的选择空间可被光滑参数化，揭示了存在一个可连续参数化的"摩擦场论模空间"。

Result: 研究发现存在一个摩擦场论模空间，其中每个点对应形状与尺度联络动力学的不同划分方式。在该模空间中移动可在不同Herglotz拉格朗日量之间转换，这些拉格朗日量的在壳动力学都一致地再现一次Palatini引力，其区别仅在于原始理论的尺度动力学如何在行动依赖与动力学挠率之间被分配。

Conclusion: SO(1,3)规范联络的分解并非唯一而是构成一个光滑参数空间，这导致存在一个摩擦场论模空间。不同的分解方案在物理上等价（都在壳再现Palatini引力），但通过行动依赖与动力学挠率的不同划分体现了理论结构的非平凡自由度。

Abstract: Previous work has shown that first-order Palatini gravity may be cast as a non-conservative Herglotz Lagrangian field theory, which excludes the conformal mode from its space of dynamical variables. In the present work, we analyse the role of the $SO(1,3)$ gauge connection in the construction of this action-dependent theory. The connection may be decomposed into `shape' and `scale' pieces, where only the former is retained within the Herglotz theory, while the latter is incorporated into the action-dependent sector. It is shown that the choice of connection decomposition is a space that can be smoothly parameterised. Such an observation leads to the finding that there exists a `moduli space of frictional field theories', in which each point corresponds to a distinct splitting of the shape and scale connection dynamics. Movement within the moduli space transitions between Herglotz Lagrangians whose on-shell dynamics all reproduce first-order Palatini gravity. The distinction lies in how the reproduction of the scale dynamics of the original theory is partitioned between action dependence and dynamical torsion.

</details>


### [106] [Non-minimally coupled Weyl connection gravity in the Solar System and at the Galactic Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24491)
*Mohsen Khodadi,Margarida Lima,Cláudio Gomes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小耦合Weyl联络引力的唯象可行性，通过将其静态球对称黑洞解与太阳系弱场经典测试及银心Sgr A*附近恒星轨道观测对比，推导出两个Schwarzschild类真空解族中自由参数ω的下限约束，并发现不同解族因修正项标度不同（1/ω vs 1/ω²）而给出显著不同的约束强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证非最小耦合Weyl联络引力这一几何框架是否与现有引力观测数据相容。在该框架中，非度规性通过Weyl矢量场编码，产生两类带长度量纲参数ω的Schwarzschild类真空解。需要通过经典弱场测试和实际天文观测来检验该理论的唯象可行性，并确定参数ω的物理允许范围。

Method: 选取两类Weyl联络引力真空解（解I为纯径向Weyl矢量，解II为时间-径向Weyl矢量），分别计算四个经典可观测量（引力红移、水星近日点进动、光线偏折、雷达回波延迟）的相对论修正；利用太阳系观测数据推导ω下界；并结合S2恒星轨道观测验证银心尺度的约束。

Result: 解I的度量修正以1/ω标度，近日点进动测试给出ω≳10^30 m的最强约束；解II的修正以1/ω²标度，同一测试给出ω≳10^20 m的较弱约束。差异源于两类解线性修正项行为不同：解I的线性项2r/ω未被抑制且在太阳系尺度占主导，解II的线性项额外被M/ω因子抑制，使得二次项-r²/4ω²在全区域占主导。S2恒星轨道观测为两类解提供了互补约束。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合Weyl联络引力的两类解在太阳系和银心尺度均可与当前观测相容，但需取足够大的参数ω。解I受约束远强于解II，反映了两个解族在扰动结构上的本质差异。该工作为这一引力理论的参数空间提供了观测层面的约束框架。

Abstract: We explore the phenomenological viability of non-minimally coupled Weyl connection gravity by confronting its static, spherically symmetric black hole solutions with classical weak-field Solar System tests and stellar-orbit observations near Sgr A*. In this geometric framework, non-metricity is encoded via a Weyl vector field, giving rise to two distinct families of Schwarzschild-like vacuum solutions characterized by a free parameter \(ω\) with dimensions of length. We compute the corrections to four classical observables---gravitational redshift, Mercury's perihelion advance, light deflection, and radar echo delay---and derive stringent lower bounds on \(ω\) using current observational data. For Solution I (purely radial Weyl vector), the leading metric corrections scale as \(1/ω\), yielding bounds as strong as \(ω\gtrsim 10^{30}\) m from perihelion precession. For Solution II (time-radial Weyl vector), the corrections scale as \(1/ω^2\), resulting in weaker constraints, with \(ω\gtrsim 10^{20}\) m from the same test. This marked difference arises from the distinct behavior of the linear corrections in each solution: while Solution I exhibits an unsuppressed linear term \(2r/ω\) that dominates in the Solar System regime, Solution II features a linear term suppressed by an additional factor of \(M/ω\), making the quadratic term \(-r^2/4ω^2\) dominant throughout. We then analyze stellar orbits near the Galactic Center, finding that current observations of the S2 star provide complementary constraints on both solutions. (...)

</details>


### [107] [Hair or a Boundary Source? Covariant Phase Space of a Static Type-I Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24511)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究一种由电磁种子通过非线性解生成映射得到的双参数静态I型真空黑洞，发现其Komar质量变化与熵变化不相容。利用Iyer-Wald、Barnich-Brandt和Lee-Wald构造，证明表面荷一形式具有非零旋度，通过在Weyl边界上的辛通量也能度量该障碍。引入场依赖的Killing场归一化作为积分因子，得到能量H=m/(1+B²m²)^{3/2}，并以Schwarzschild质量归一化唯一选定。该力学满足全局形式dM=TdS+Ψ_y dy，表明B参数编码真实的几何形变，其变化在固定边界时对应的哈密顿量中表现为外部源做功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双参数静态黑洞中，Komar质量变化与熵变化之间存在不相容性，这构成了热力学第一定律形式上的障碍。需要理解该障碍的几何与辛结构来源，并寻找恰当的物理量定义以恢复一致的力学关系。

Method: 采用Iyer-Wald、Barnich-Brandt与Lee-Wald三种协变相空间构造推导表面荷一形式；分析其旋度及Weyl边界处的辛通量；通过场依赖的Killing场归一化引入积分因子；结合Brown-York拟局域方法分析空间无穷远的正则响应；在Weyl坐标中研究极限几何。

Result: 表面荷一形式在整个正则解空间上具有非零旋度，同一障碍可由穿过Weyl边界的辛通量度量。场依赖归一化给出能量H=m/(1+B²m²)^{3/2}，由Schwarzschild质量归一化唯一确定。固定G的相空间在标度变换下保持二维，Brown-York响应逐点复现Lee-Wald通量。全局力学关系为dM=TdS+Ψ_y dy。

Conclusion: 参数B代表真实的几何形变而非纯规范或冗余参数；其变化在固定边界时间关联的哈密顿形式中充当外部源做功项。这一结果调和了Komar质量与熵变化之间的视在矛盾，并为理解非渐近平坦黑洞热力学的形式结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study the two-parameter static type-I vacuum black hole recently obtained from an electromagnetic seed by a nonlinear solution-generating map. Its parameter $B$ changes dimensionless horizon geometry, yet the Komar mass variation at fixed asymptotic time is incompatible with the entropy variation. Using the Iyer--Wald, Barnich--Brandt, and Lee--Wald constructions, we derive the surface-charge one-form throughout the regular solution space and show that it has a nonzero curl. The same obstruction is measured by symplectic flux through the limiting Weyl boundary. A field-dependent normalization of the stationary Killing field supplies an integrating factor; the resulting energy $H=m/(1+B^2m^2)^{3/2}$ is uniquely selected by Schwarzschild mass normalization within this class. Homothetic scaling leaves the fixed-$G$ phase space two-dimensional because its tangent carries nonzero exact-symmetry charges. In Weyl coordinates, spatial infinity at finite mass is a closed surface of revolution. Its Brown--York canonical response reproduces the Lee--Wald flux pointwise. The semi-infinite annular source of the massless background emerges as a nonuniform limit of this surface. Finally, with $y=Bm$, the mechanics takes the global form $\text{d} M=T\text{d} S+Ψ_y\text{d} y$. Thus $B$ labels a genuine geometric deformation while its variation acts as external-source work in the Hamiltonian associated with fixed boundary time.

</details>


### [108] [Efficient time-domain eccentric model for Galactic binaries in LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24546)
*Shubhanshu Tiwari,Johan O. A. Robertsson,A. Gopakumar,Michael Ebersold,Fredrik P. Andersson,Maria Haney,Philippe Jetzer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了eGB-multi，一个用于LISA任务的快速时域偏心银河双星波形软件包。该软件包结合了后牛顿精度准开普勒偏心源模型和时域LISA响应，提供从牛顿闭合偏心轨道到包含引力辐射反作用的进动收缩偏心轨道等多级轨道描述，并量化了轨道偏心率使常见准圆近似失效的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河双星是LISA数据流中最丰富的长寿命信号源，通常被建模为近圆、近单色系统。然而实际银河双星可能具有非零偏心率，现有的准圆近似可能不再适用。因此需要一个能够灵活处理不同复杂度轨道动力学的快速波形生成工具，以评估偏心率对波形建模的影响。

Method: eGB-multi软件包采用模块化实现：将后牛顿精度下的准开普勒偏心源模型与LISA时域响应相结合，在延迟单程链路上评估信号并构造时延干涉（TDI）观测量。它支持从牛顿闭合偏心轨道、含1PN近星点进动的相对论进动偏心轨道，到额外计入领先四极阶引力辐射反作用的进动收缩偏心轨道等多级描述选项，并提供基于预设失配容差的自动演化模式。

Result: 利用该框架量化了轨道偏心率导致常用准圆近似对银河双星不充分的参数区间，明确了在何种偏心率和频率条件下需要采用完整的偏心波形模型。

Conclusion: eGB-multi为LISA银河双星信号分析提供了一个灵活、快速且精度可选的偏心波形生成框架，可用于指导偏心双星信号的搜索与参数估计，并评估准圆近似的适用范围。

Abstract: Galactic binaries are expected to be the most numerous long-lived sources in the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) data stream and are commonly modeled as quasi-circular, nearly monochromatic systems. In this work we present \texttt{eGB-multi}, a fast time-domain eccentric Galactic-binary waveform package for LISA. The package combines a post-Newtonian-accurate quasi-Keplerian eccentric source model with a time-domain LISA response, evaluating the signal on the retarded one-way links and then constructing time-delay interferometry observables. Its modular implementation allows the user to switch between different levels of orbital description, ranging from Newtonian closed eccentric orbits and relativistically precessing eccentric orbits incorporating the 1PN-accurate periastron advance, to precessing and shrinking eccentric orbits that additionally account for gravitational radiation reaction at leading quadrupolar order. The framework also provides an automatic evolution mode, in which the appropriate level of orbital dynamics is selected based on a pre-defined mismatch tolerance. We employ this framework to quantify the regimes in which orbital eccentricity renders the commonly adopted quasi-circular approximation for Galactic binaries inadequate.

</details>


### [109] [Revisit Weak Cosmic Censorship in Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton Black Holes: Third-order Protection, Swampland Distance Conjecture, and Weak Gravity Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24557)
*Sheng-Hong Lai,Wen-Yu Wen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量(EMD)理论中，对静态带电GMG黑洞进行了完整的高阶Sorce-Wald思想实验，计算了三阶变分恒等式。结果表明极值参数η在三阶微扰阶内保持非负，确认了弱宇宙监督猜想(WCCC)的保护作用；同时引入Swampland距离猜想(SDC)，指出无穷轻带电态塔会在远小于2M的时间尺度上中和任何可能的过度带电构型，极大地放宽了原始弱引力猜想(WGC)的荷质比界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作在probe极限下发现极值GMG黑洞可能被测试粒子过度带电，看似违反WCCC；该窗口可通过Hoop猜想或二阶变分恒等式闭合。本文动机是进一步计算三阶变分恒等式，以更完整地检验WCCC，并引入SDC来在动力学机制中强化WCCC并放宽WGC约束。

Method: 作者在Iyer-Wald形式主义框架下，对极值GMG黑洞执行完整的Sorce-Wald思想实验，显式计算三阶变分恒等式，以检验极值参数η在第三阶微扰下的非负性。同时结合Swampland距离猜想，分析无穷轻带电态塔对任何过度带电构型的中和时间尺度。

Result: 三阶微扰阶内极值参数η保持非负，确认了WCCC的保护，即使Lu等人的全阶推广的模型无关证明看似失效。SDC的引入表明带电态塔在远小于2M的时间尺度上中和过度带电构型，指数级快于视界被破坏的时间；这大幅放宽了原始WGC的荷质比界限。

Conclusion: WCCC在GMG黑洞三阶微扰及其动态机制中得到确认；结合SDC后，WGC的荷质比下限被显著放宽，表明极值黑洞的稳定性与Swampland猜想之间存在深层联系。

Abstract: We extend the earlier analysis of cosmic censorship and the Weak Gravity Conjecture (WGC) in Einstein--Maxwell-dilaton (EMD) theory by performing a complete higher-order Sorce--Wald gedankenexperiment on the static charged Gibbons--Maeda--Garfinkle--Horowitz--Strominger (GMG) black hole. In the probe limit, an extremal GMG black hole can apparently be overcharged by a test particle, seemingly violating the weak cosmic censorship conjecture (WCCC), which is nothing but first-order variational identity in the Iyer--Wald formalism. This window can be closed by either incorporating backreaction via the Hoop Conjecture or further including second-order variational identities. In this paper, we explicitly compute third-order variational identities. The extremality parameter eta remains non-negative to the third perturbative orders confirming WCCC protection, regardless of the apparent failure of all-order generalization, model-independent proof of Lu, et al.. We further incorporate the Swampland Distance Conjecture (SDC): the resulting infinite tower of light charged states discharges any would-be overcharged configuration on a timescale tau much less than 2M, exponentially faster than a horizon can be destroyed, reinforcing WCCC in dynamical regimes and strongly relaxing the original WGC charge-to-mass bound.

</details>


### [110] [Classification of Maximally Charged Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24843)
*Sven Hirsch,Yiyue Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在3+1维时空中完整刻画了所有最大电荷黑洞时空：任何饱和质量-电荷不等式且满足带电主导能量条件的初始数据集，必然是Majumdar-Papapetrou时空的等距嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量-电荷不等式是广义相对论中带电黑洞的重要研究工具，但其等号情形（即饱和情形）的几何刻画问题此前尚未完全解决。理解等号成立时的时空结构对于黑洞唯一性、引力稳定性等问题具有根本意义。

Method: 作者采用几何分析的方法，利用带电主导能量条件（charged dominant energy condition）和初始数据集的约束方程，通过证明饱和质量-电荷不等式的初始数据必须满足刚性条件，从而将其等距嵌入到Majumdar-Papapetrou时空（即多个极端充电黑洞的静态平衡构型）中。

Result: 证明了在3+1维中，凡饱和质量-电荷不等式且满足带电主导能量条件的初始数据集，都必须来自Majumdar-Papapetrou时空的等距嵌入。这给出了最大电荷黑洞时空的完整分类刻画。

Conclusion: 这项工作完成了带电情形下质量-能量不等式的刚性刻画（rigidity），表明最大电荷黑洞的几何结构本质上由Majumdar-Papapetrou解唯一决定，深化了我们对极端带电黑洞时空结构的理解。

Abstract: We characterize all maximally charged black hole spacetimes in $3+1$ dimensions. More precisely, given any initial data set $(M,g,k)$ saturating the mass-charge inequality that satisfies the charged dominant energy condition, we show that $(M,g,k)$ must arise from an isometric embedding into a Majumdar-Papapetrou spacetime.

</details>


### [111] [The Bondi Dipole in Full Numerical Relativity: a Self-Accelerating Positive-Negative Mass Binary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24577)
*Nikita M. Shirokov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在3+1数值相对论框架下首次研究了"Bondi偶极子"（负主动引力质量体）的动力学行为，通过34次数值演化证实了负引力质量星体与正质量星体组成的质量匹配对从静止释放后会自加速逃逸，并系统验证了其引力标度律、辐射特性及在数值处理下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bondi早在1957年就从理论层面指出，具有相反主动引力质量的物体间存在自加速效应（负质量追逐正质量、相互排斥并一起逃逸）。然而这一理论预言长期未在数值相对论层面得到验证。作者旨在通过现代数值相对论工具，首次实现对负主动引力质量系统（Bondi偶极子）的实际演化，从而检验其动力学行为、辐射特性以及数值稳定性，并填补负ADM质量天体数值研究的空白。

Method: 作者构建了包含两个复标量场的系统：两者共享相同的Klein-Gordon动力学，但其中一个"幻影"标量（phantom）以负号进入爱因斯坦方程。由此实现惯性质量和被动质量为正、而主动质量为负的配置。在35（34次演化）矩阵中，对释放于静止状态的质量匹配对进行3+1数值相对论演化，涵盖逃逸对、对照组和参数扫描。研究过程中系统测量了中点的位移、分离度保持、加速度及其标度律（对分离距离d、质量M的依赖），并检验了圆极矩、八极矩的辐射特征、扇形交换、规范/求解器/网格等数值变量对结果的影响。

Result: （1）质量匹配的Bondi偶极对从静止释放后作为一个整体自加速：中点在t=200时移动3.00±0.01（分离度保持1%以内），t=400时速度达0.056c且加速度稳定在2%以内，总符号动量保持为零（不确定性≤1%）。这证实了Bondi的理论预言。（2）引力在两条作用方向上均满足标度律：a∝d^(−2.03±0.01)（d=8~20），a∝M^(0.97±0.06)（质量变化2.5倍），等质量阶梯上ad²/M̄=1（偏差2.4%以内）。（3）数值稳健性：交换扇形使加速度反转，精度达2×10⁻⁵；规范、求解器深度和网格变化对漂移影响≤0.01%，盒子加倍影响4%；同号控制对的质心保持≤8×10⁻⁴。该逃逸对无可探测的引力辐射：符号偶极子不能辐射，四极子的ẍ常定，ℓ=2振幅随r^(−4.8)衰减（近场而非通量）。（4）幻影星体是数值相对论中首个演化出的负ADM质量渐近平直天体，单独存活至t=1000，缓慢向外弛豫。

Conclusion: 本文首次在数值相对论中成功演化出负ADM质量的渐近平直天体（幻影星体），并精确验证了Bondi偶极子的自加速逃逸行为：质量匹配对从静止释放后以恒定加速度作为一个整体加速，其动力学严格符合牛顿引力平方反比标度律，且不产生引力辐射（因符号偶极子无法辐射）。研究同时证实该系统的数值行为高度稳定，对规范、求解器、网格及盒尺寸等数值设置不敏感。这项工作将Bondi的经典预言从理论推导推进到数值验证，为负引力质量的引力动力学研究提供了首个可靠的数值基准。

Abstract: Bondi showed in 1957 that bodies of opposite active gravitational mass self-accelerate: the negative chases the positive it repels, and the pair runs off together. We evolve this "Bondi dipole" in $3+1$ numerical relativity: two complex scalars share identical Klein-Gordon dynamics; only the phantom enters Einstein's equations with a minus sign -- inertial and passive masses positive, active mass negative. Across a matrix of thirty-four evolutions -- runaway pairs, controls, and parameter scans -- a mass-matched pair released at rest accelerates as a unit. The midpoint moves $3.00\pm0.01$ by $t=200$ with the separation held to 1%, and reaches a speed of $0.056c$ by $t=400$ with the acceleration steady to 2%; the total signed momentum holds at zero to $\lesssim 1$%. The force is gravity on both of its axes: $a\propto d^{-2.03\pm0.01}$ over $d=8$ to $20$, $a\propto M^{0.97\pm0.06}$ over a factor $2.5$ in mass, and $a\,d^2/\bar{M}=1$ within 2.4% on the equal-mass ladder. Swapping the sectors inverts the acceleration to two parts in $10^5$; gauge, solver-depth and mesh variations move the drift by $\lesssim 0.01$% and box doubling by 4%; same-sign control pairs hold their centroids to $\lesssim 8\times10^{-4}$ even while merging. The runaway carries no detectable gravitational radiation: the signed dipole cannot radiate, the quadrupole's $\ddot{Q}$ is constant, and the measured $\ell=2$ amplitude falls as $r^{-4.8}$ -- near zone, not flux. The phantom star is, to our knowledge, the first asymptotically flat body of negative ADM mass evolved in numerical relativity; alone it survives to $t=1000$, slowly relaxing outward.

</details>


### [112] [Global uniqueness of Kerr black hole spin and inclination from a segment of the critical curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24635)
*Kenta Hioki*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于非极端旋转Kerr黑洞（0<a<1，0<i≤π/2），观测屏幕上临界曲线（critical curve）的任意连通正长度片段，在位置和方向未知的情况下，可以唯一确定黑洞的自旋参数a和观测倾角i。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率观测有望探测黑洞图像的环形结构（光子环），而临界曲线是更高阶光子亚环在观测屏幕上累积的极限。理解该曲线的几何形状与Kerr黑洞参数（自旋a和倾角i）之间的关系，是反演黑洞参数的关键。然而，实际观测只能获得临界曲线的一个片段，且其位置和方向未知，因此需要证明参数唯一可辨识性。

Method: 证明分为两步：(1) 利用不可约隐式多项式，证明临界曲线的一个连通正长度片段能唯一确定包含它的完整代数曲线；(2) 从该多项式的刚体运动不变量中恢复参数对(a,i)。通过构造不可约隐式多项式来排除不同参数产生相同曲线片段的歧义，并结合刚体运动的性质完成全局唯一性证明。

Result: 证明了全局唯一性定理：对于0<a<1且0<i≤π/2的非极端旋转Kerr黑洞，如果两个临界曲线片段经过保向刚体运动后作为点集重合，则它们的Kerr参数对必然相同，且两个刚体运动完全相同。

Conclusion: 该结果表明临界曲线的任意可观测片段即可唯一确定Kerr黑洞的自旋参数和倾角，为未来高分辨率黑洞成像观测（如Event Horizon Telescope等）的参数反演提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: High-resolution observations are expected to probe the photon-ring structure of black hole images. The critical curve is the geometrically defined limiting locus on the observer's screen toward which successive higher-order photon subrings accumulate. For a Kerr black hole, the shape of this limiting curve depends on the dimensionless spin parameter $a$ and the inclination angle $i$. We investigate whether these parameters can be determined uniquely from a connected positive-length segment of the critical curve when the segment's position and orientation on the screen are unknown. For rotating non-extremal Kerr black holes with $0<a<1$ and $0<i\leqπ/2$, we prove global uniqueness: if two such segments coincide as point sets after orientation-preserving rigid motions of the screen, then their Kerr parameter pairs coincide and the two rigid motions are identical. The proof uses an irreducible implicit polynomial to show that a segment determines the full algebraic curve containing it and then recovers the parameter pair $(a,i)$ from rigid-motion invariants of that polynomial.

</details>


### [113] [Inhomogeneous Cosmologies with Bumpy Structure from Superfluid Pionic Vortices and the propagation of electromagnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24666)
*Fabrizio Canfora,Alex Giacomini,Nikolaos Dimakis,Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在(3+1)维广义相对论框架下引入了同时包含常规流体分量和超流体分量（描述超流体Pionic涡旋）的精确宇宙学解。这些超流体涡旋作为不均匀性来源可以被精确处理，无需微扰理论，为宇宙学中不均匀性的非微扰描述提供了解析解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宇宙学对不均匀性的处理通常依赖微扰理论，但微扰方法在强耦合或大尺度不均匀情况下可能失效。本文旨在通过构造精确解析解，获得宇宙学中不均匀性的非微扰描述，从而更准确地理解超流体Pionic涡旋这类强子不均匀性对宇宙演化的影响及其可观测效应。

Method: 在广义相对论(3+1)维框架下，引入包含常规流体分量和超流体分量（描述超流体Pionic涡旋）的能量-动量张量，构造精确的宇宙学解。该解无需微扰理论即可精确处理超流体涡旋引起的不均匀性，并通过分析电磁场在这些宇宙学背景上的传播来探讨可能的可观测效应，同时讨论有效超流体为保证解存在所需满足的性质及解的推广方向。

Result: 获得了包含常规流体与超流体Pionic涡旋的精确宇宙学解析解，实现了对宇宙学不均匀性的非微扰描述。通过分析电磁场在上述背景中的传播，识别了这些强子不均匀性可能产生的可观测效应，并给出了有效超流体在该类解存在时所需满足的性质条件。

Conclusion: 这项工作为宇宙学不均匀性提供了非微扰的解析描述工具，展示了超流体Pionic涡旋作为不均匀性来源的特殊物理效应及其潜在的可观测信号，并为该族解的未来扩展留下了空间，对理解强子物质在宇宙学尺度的作用具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this work we introduce exact cosmological solutions in General Relativity in (3+1) dimensions which take into account both the usual fluid component as well as a superfluid component describing superfluid Pionic vortices. Such superfluid vortices are source of inhomogeneities which can be treated exactly, no perturbation theory is required. These analytic solutions are relevant to obtain a non-perturbative description of inhomogeneities in cosmology. We discuss the peculiar effects of these hadronic inhomogeneities on the propagation of electromagnetic fields on such cosmological backgrounds analyzing possible observable effects. Finally, the properties that the effective superfluid satisfy for such solutions to exist are discussed, together with possible extensions of this family of solutions.

</details>


### [114] [Testing $f(Q)$ Gravity with DESI DR2 and Strong-Lensing Time Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24676)
*Darshan Kumar,Saibal Ray,Fengge Zhang,Nisha Rani,Praveen Kumar Dhankar,Jie Zheng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个f(Q)引力模型（归一化幂律模型和平方根指数模型）能否描述晚期宇宙加速膨胀，通过MCMC分析结合多种观测数据（宇宙计时器、DESI DR2重子声学振荡、强引力透镜时间延迟及三个Ia型超新星样本），发现平方根指数模型在统计上优于ΛCDM，归一化幂律模型与ΛCDM竞争但额外参数不被信息准则支持，两模型的减速-加速转变红移与观测一致，支持f(Q)引力作为无需宇宙学常数的可行替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称遥平行引力（f(Q)引力）提供了无需宇宙学常数即可解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀的替代几何描述，本文旨在检验两个f(Q)模型对晚期膨胀历史的描述能力，并与标准ΛCDM模型进行统计比较。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）分析，利用宇宙计时器（CC）测量、DESI DR2重子声学振荡（BAO）、强引力透镜时间延迟观测，以及三个Ia型超新星样本（Pantheon+、Union 3.0和DES Y5）约束两个f(Q)模型的参数；通过最小χ²、赤池信息准则（AIC）和贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）与平坦ΛCDM模型进行统计比较。

Result: 平方根指数模型在CC+DESI DR2+强透镜时间延迟数据与Pantheon+和Union 3.0超新星组合下提供了比ΛCDM更好的统计拟合，在拟合优度和信息准则上均有改进；归一化幂律模型在含超新星的数据组合中与ΛCDM统计上竞争，但其额外参数不被信息准则支持。两个模型确定的减速-加速转变红移在不同数据组合下保持一致，且与观测估计相符。

Conclusion: f(Q)引力可以作为ΛCDM的可行替代方案来解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀，无需引入宇宙学常数；其中平方根指数模型在统计表现上尤其具有竞争力。

Abstract: Symmetric teleparallel gravity provides an alternative description of gravitation in which non-metricity replaces curvature and torsion. Its extension through $f(Q)$ gravity offers a different geometric description of the late-time expansion of the Universe and its accelerated phase. In this work, we investigate two $f(Q)$ models, a normalized power-law model and a square-root exponential model, and test their ability to describe the late-time expansion history. We constrain the model parameters through Markov chain Monte Carlo analyses using Cosmic Chronometer measurements, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations, strong-lensing time-delay observations, and three Type Ia supernova compilations, Pantheon$^+$, Union 3.0, and DES Y5. We compare both models with the flat $Λ$CDM model using the minimum $χ^2$, Akaike information criterion, and Bayesian information criterion. The square-root exponential model provides a better statistical fit than $Λ$CDM for the combinations of Cosmic Chronometer, DESI DR2, and strong-lensing time-delay data with Pantheon$^+$ and Union 3.0, with improvements in both the goodness of fit and information criteria. The normalized power-law model remains statistically competitive with $Λ$CDM for the supernova-inclusive combinations, although the information criteria do not favor its additional parameter. We also determine the transition redshift from cosmic deceleration to acceleration for both models, obtaining consistent values across the different dataset combinations. The transition redshifts agree with observational estimates of the cosmic acceleration epoch. Overall, our results support $f(Q)$ gravity as a viable alternative to $Λ$CDM for explaining the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe without requiring a cosmological constant.

</details>


### [115] [Unsupervised Deep Learning Method for Clustering KAGRA O3GK Transient Noise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24690)
*Yubin Kim,Kihyun Jung,Kyujin Kwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了使用无监督深度学习方法对KAGRA引力波探测器O3GK运行期间的瞬态噪声（glitch）频谱图进行降维和聚类分类，发现深度学习的特征提取显著优于传统机器学习方法，为可扩展的引力波数据噪声分类提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器会受到各种瞬态噪声（glitch）的影响，这些噪声会模仿真实引力波信号并限制探测器灵敏度。传统的有监督机器学习方法虽然常用于glitch分类，但需要大量人工标注的训练数据，成本高昂且无法适应探测器的持续演化。因此，需要探索无需标注的无监督深度学习方法来实现可扩展的glitch分类。

Method: 使用KAGRA探测器O3GK运行数据，应用三种不同的无监督深度学习方法对glitch频谱图图像进行降维和聚类，并与传统机器学习方法进行性能对比。

Result: 基于深度学习的特征提取方法相比传统机器学习方法显著提升了glitch聚类性能。这是首次对KAGRA glitch数据集进行无监督深度学习的分析。

Conclusion: 无监督深度学习方法在glitch分类中展现出高效的潜力，能够克服有监督方法依赖人工标注的局限性，为未来观测运行中可扩展的glitch分类提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The advent of ground-based gravitational wave detectors has significantly improved the detection of faint gravitational wave signals. However, these detectors are affected by various types of transient noise, known as glitches, which can mimic true gravitational wave signals and limit detector sensitivity. Classifying glitches according to their time-frequency characteristics not only facilitates a deeper understanding of their origins, but also supports their mitigation on data to maintain data quality. While supervised machine learning methods are commonly employed for glitch classification, they require the labelling of training datasets obtained through manual annotation, a process which is costly and not scalable for evolving detectors. In order to address this challenge, the present study investigates unsupervised deep learning methods for dimensionality reduction and clustering of glitch spectrogram images. In this study, three distinct approaches are applied and compared using data from the KAGRA detector's O3GK run. The findings of this study demonstrate that deep learning-based feature extraction significantly enhances the clustering performance compared to traditional machine learning methods. This study presents an initial analysis of the KAGRA glitch dataset using unsupervised deep learning, highlighting the potential of this approach for efficient and scalable glitch classification in future observation runs.

</details>


### [116] [Gravitational Lensing of Gravitational Wave for Generalized Navarro-Frenk-White Profile and Einasto Profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24772)
*Hanyu Jiang,Xiao Guo,Youjun Lu,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种代表性暗物质晕密度轮廓（广义Navarro-Frenk-White轮廓gNFW和Einasto轮廓）对引力波透镜效应的影响，分析了强透镜下的参数空间分布和成像特征，并详细考察了低频区放大因子F(w,y)的模和相位特性。研究发现gNFW坡度γ增大会导致振幅更大、振荡更快、波包形态更明显；且在相同条件下Einasto轮廓的透镜效应更强，其频率演化行为与gNFW有明显差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质晕的密度轮廓携带暗物质本质的印记，通过引力波透镜效应可以约束暗物质性质。研究不同密度轮廓对引力波透镜的影响，有助于利用引力波透镜现象来区分和约束暗物质模型。

Method: 基于引力透镜方程，对gNFW和Einasto两种密度轮廓进行数值分析。首先在强透镜条件下考察参数空间分布和成像特性，根据Morse指数划分参数空间区域；然后重点分析低频区域放大因子F(w,y)的模|F|和相位Arg(F)的行为。

Result: 固定透镜质量时，增大gNFW的坡度参数γ会使振幅增大、振荡更频繁、多像区域的波包形态更显著。在α=0.16、y<0.6且具有相同M_{200}的条件下，Einasto轮廓的透镜效应强于gNFW轮廓。Einasto轮廓的F(w,y)随频率的演化行为与gNFW情况不同，表现出独特特征。

Conclusion: 不同暗物质密度轮廓在引力波透镜中呈现可区分的信号特征，特别是Einasto轮廓的独特频率演化行为可作为区分不同暗物质密度轮廓的观测标志，为未来利用引力波透镜约束暗物质性质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The density profiles of Dark matter (DM) halos carry imprints of the DM nature and may be constrained through the lensing effects on gravitational waves (GWs) arising from the halo gravitational potential. In this paper, we investigate GW lensing by two representative types of halo density profiles, i.e., the generalized Navarro-Frenk-White (gNFW) density profile and the Einasto density profile. Using the gravitational lensing equation, we first examine the parameter-space distribution and the imaging characteristics of both profiles under strong lensing, partitioning the parameter space into distinct regions according to the Morse index. We then conduct a detailed analysis of the modulus $\big|F\big|$ and phase $\mathrm{Arg}(F)$ of the amplification factor $F\big(w,y\big)$ at low frequency regime. Our results show that, for a fixed lens mass , increasing the gNFW slope $γ$ leads to a larger amplitude, more rapid oscillation, and more distinct wave-packet morphology in the multiple-image regime. Compared with the gNFW case, the Einasto case (with $α=0.16$, $y<0.6$, and the same $M_{200}$) produces a stronger lensing effect. Notably, the evolution of $F\big(w,y\big)$ with frequency for the Einasto profile differs from that of the gNFW case, making its behavior particularly distinctive.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamical Friction as Environmental Gravitational Self-Force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24800)
*Sayak Datta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明动力学摩擦（DF）并非独立于引力波辐射反作用力的额外耗散机制，而是MiSaTaQuWa自力方程在协变多参数展开中q²ε阶的项。在弱场绝热极限下，该识别可复现Chandrasekhar-Ostriker拖曳力，并预测强引力下的两项新特征，从而将动力学摩擦确立为自力理论的内在组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，动力学摩擦与引力波辐射反作用被视为作用于致密天体在物质环境中旋进时的两种独立耗散机制。本文试图澄清二者的关系，并证明动力学摩擦本质上属于自力理论（self-force theory）的固有部分，而非附加的独立作用力，这对于极端质量比旋进（EMRI）引力波波形建模具有直接影响。

Method: 在协变量子（多参数）展开框架下，作者以质量比q和环境参数ε为展开参数，证明DF项在粒子对环境物质场不存在诱导扰动时恒为零。作为验证，他们在弱场和绝热极限下求解由粒子自身q阶真空度规扰动源驱动的极向密度扰动方程，通过对该方程的分析来检验自力的识别。

Result: 该框架在弱场极限下成功复现了Chandrasekhar-Ostriker拖曳力，同时预测了强引力下的两个新特征：(1) 尾流（wake）出现依赖于角量子数ℓ的劈裂，仅在弱场极限下才重组为Ostriker源；(2) 存在一个纯相对论的轴向量贡献，在牛顿极限下没有对应物。

Conclusion: 动力学摩擦是自力理论的固有组成部分，可自然推广到强场、一般轨道和一般环境。该统一框架对LISA探测的极端质量比旋进引力波波形建模具有直接意义。

Abstract: Dynamical friction (DF) and gravitational-wave radiation reaction are conventionally treated as distinct dissipative mechanisms acting on a compact object inspiraling through a matter environment. We show that DF is not an additional force but a term at order $q^2ε$ in a covariant multiparameter expansion of the MiSaTaQuWa force equation that vanishes identically in the absence of a particle-induced perturbation of the environment's matter fields, where $q$ and $ε$ are respectively the mass ratio and environmental parameter. As a test of this identification, we evaluate it in weak and adiabatic field limit, solving the polar density-perturbation equation sourced by the particle's own order-$q$ vacuum metric perturbation. The reduction reproduces the Chandrasekhar-Ostriker drag force and predicts two features in strong gravity. An $\ell$-dependent splitting of the wake that only recombines into the Ostriker source in the weak-field limit, and a purely relativistic axial contribution with no Newtonian counterpart. This establishes dynamical friction as an intrinsic piece of self-force theory, extending to strong field, generic orbits, and generic environments, with direct consequences for extreme-mass-ratio inspiral waveform modeling for LISA.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [The $J$-equation on Kähler manifolds under a smooth boundary cone condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23747)
*Junbang Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致Kähler流形上关于Kähler类对(α,β)的J-零轨迹与J-非ample轨迹的等价性，在J-大性假设和边界锥条件下，利用推广的Khovanskii-Teissier不等式进行证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复几何中，理解J方程相关的代数与解析几何对象之间的关系是重要问题。作者希望证明在适当的假设下，代数定义的J-零轨迹与解析定义的J-非ample轨迹重合，从而建立代数与解析之间的桥梁。

Method: 利用Collins提出的与J方程相关的广义Khovanskii-Teissier不等式，并将其推广到奇异Kähler空间上，结合J-大性假设和边界锥条件进行分析。

Result: 在J-大性条件下（对三维以下半稳定对自动成立）以及边界锥条件成立时，代数定义的J-零轨迹等于解析定义的J-非ample轨迹。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了J-零轨迹在代数与解析两个层面的等价性，深化了对Kähler流形上J方程及其相关几何结构的理解。

Abstract: For a $n$-dimensional compact Kähler manifold $X$ with a pair of Kähler classes $(α,β)$, we show that the algebraically defined $J$-null locus is equal to the analytically defined $J$-non-ample locus under the assumption of $J$-bigness(which is automatic for semistable pair up to dimension $3$ \cite{3d}), and boundary cone condition $c_{α,β}ω^{n-1}-(n-1)ω^{n-2}\wedgeχ\geq 0$ for some Kähler form $ω\in α,χ\in β$. The key tool is the generalized Khovanskii-Teissier inequality associated to $J$ equation formulated by Collins \cite{CT21} and its extension to singular Kähler space.

</details>


### [119] [Liouville theorems and Evans-Krylov estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23772)
*Yifan Chen,Hans-Joachim Hein,Ryan Mc Gowan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该课程论文回顾了Simon（1997）关于Liouville定理与Schauder型正则性估计等价性的经典思想，并将其应用于复Monge-Ampère方程在奇异参考度量下的正则性研究。文中先回顾Calabi-Aubin-Yau的经典$C^2$和$C^3$估计，再给出Euclidean球上Evans-Krylov $C^{2,\alpha}$估计的新证明，并讨论柱面和锥面等奇异背景下的近期工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复Monge-Ampère方程在奇异参考度量下的正则性问题一直是Kähler几何中的核心难点。论文旨在利用Liouville定理与Schauder估计的经典等价关系这一分析学思想，为奇异背景下的正则性估计提供统一的视角和新的证明方法。

Method: 论文采用经典分析思想框架，首先快速回顾Calabi-Aubin-Yau的经典$C^2$、$C^3$估计，然后基于Liouville型命题与Schauder估计的等价性，给出Evans-Krylov $C^{2,\alpha}$估计在Euclidean球上的新证明，并将该方法推广到柱面、锥面等奇异参考度量的情形，结合Hein、Tosatti、Lee和Klemmensen等人的近期工作展开讨论。课程还包含五道带解答的习题和一道问题列表。

Result: 论文提供了一个基于Liouville-Schauder等价思想的新证明框架，成功给出了Evans-Krylov估计的替代证明，并将这一思路扩展到奇异参考背景下复Monge-Ampère方程的正则性研究中，系统整理了该方向的最新进展。

Conclusion: 该论文（课程讲义）展示了将Liouville定理与Schauder估计的经典等价思想推广到复Monge-Ampère方程奇异参考度量情形的可行性，为该领域提供了统一的理论视角和新的证明工具，同时也列出了未解决问题以指引后续研究。

Abstract: A classical idea in analysis going back at least to work of Simon (1997) is that Liouville theorems for solutions to elliptic or parabolic PDEs are equivalent to Schauder type regularity estimates. The goal of this course is to describe some recent developments of this idea concerning the regularity of the complex Monge-Ampère equation with respect to singular reference metrics. We will start with a quick look at the classical $C^2$ and $C^3$ estimates of Calabi-Aubin-Yau and then present a new proof of the Evans-Krylov $C^{2,α}$ estimate on a Euclidean ball. Based on this we will consider the case of singular backgrounds such as cylinders and cones, discussing some recent work by Hein, Tosatti, Lee and Klemmensen. Our discussion is far from complete and knowledge of Kähler geometry including the Aubin-Yau theorems is assumed. The course includes five exercises with solutions and a problem list.

</details>


### [120] [A characterization of complex projective space via the Calabi curvature operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23778)
*Hasan M. El-Hasan,Xiaolong Li,Jan Nienhaus,Peter Petersen,James Stanfield,Matthias Wink*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Kähler-Einstein流形上的Tachibana型定理，仅基于Calabi曲率算子来刻画复射影空间，并解决了AIM研讨会"Bochner技巧"（2026年5月）中的一个未解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Tachibana定理利用截面曲率或曲率算子的正性来刻画复射影空间CP^n。本文旨在通过Calabi曲率算子这一更精细的曲率算子来获得类似的刻画结果，并回答AIM研讨会上提出的公开问题。

Method: 作者推导了一个新的Bochner公式，将Lichnerowicz曲率项通过Calabi曲率算子来表达，并利用该公式证明Tachibana型结果。

Result: 在Kähler-Einstein流形上，基于Calabi曲率算子的一种正性/刚性条件可以刻画复射影空间，从而推广了经典的Tachibana定理。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了Calabi曲率算子与Kähler-Einstein流形刚性问题之间的新联系，解决了AIM研讨会提出的公开问题。

Abstract: We prove a Tachibana-type result for Kähler-Einstein manifolds isolating complex projective space based on its Calabi curvature operator. The proof uses a new Bochner formula expressing the Lichnerowicz curvature term via the Calabi curvature operator. This resolves one of the problems posed at the AIM workshop "The Bochner Technique'' (May 2026).

</details>


### [121] [Parabolic Bogomolov--Gieseker Inequality and Pluriharmonic Metrics on Regular Parabolic Higgs Sheaves over Compact Kähler Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23787)
*Tianshu Jiang,Jiayu Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了两项主要结果：(1) 紧Kähler流形上带简单正规交叉除子的稳定正则抛物Higgs层满足抛物Bogomolov–Gieseker不等式；(2) 在特定数值条件（每个稳定直和项的抛物度为零且第二抛物Chern特征消失）下，多稳定正则抛物Higgs层在X∖D上存在多重调和度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Bogomolov–Gieseker不等式和多重调和度量存在性推广到抛物Higgs层的正则情形，为抛物Higgs束的模空间理论和几何结构研究奠定基础。

Method: 利用抛物Higgs层的稳定性和正则性条件，结合多重调和度量方程与抛物Chern特征的计算，通过解析与代数几何相结合的方法证明不等式和度量的存在性。

Result: 建立了稳定正则抛物Higgs层的抛物Bogomolov–Gieseker不等式，并在抛物度为零且第二抛物Chern特征消失的条件下，证明多稳定情形下多重调和度量的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文将Bogomolov–Gieseker不等式和多重调和度量存在性定理推广至带奇点的抛物Higgs层框架下，扩展了Hitchin–Simpson对应理论在抛物情形的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove a parabolic Bogomolov--Gieseker inequality for stable regular parabolic Higgs sheaves on a compact Kähler manifold \((X,ω)\) equipped with a simple normal crossing divisor \(D\). We also prove that a polystable regular parabolic Higgs sheaf admits a pluriharmonic metric on \(X\setminus D\), provided that each stable summand has parabolic degree zero and vanishing integrated second parabolic Chern character.

</details>


### [122] [Nonorientable Minimal Surfaces Embedded in the Round $4$-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23890)
*Mikhail Karpukhin,Robert Kusner,Peter McGrath,Daniel Stern*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明每个闭的非可定向曲面都可以极小嵌入到4维球面S^4中，首次给出了具有负欧拉特征数的非可定向极小曲面例子。这些曲面面积均小于8π，对Willmore泛函极小化问题有应用。非可定向亏格的几何不同嵌入数随亏格至少指数增长。其中一族高度对称曲面在大概率极限下收敛到四个半球面沿大圆相接的结构，其缩放极限给出R^4中新的单周期非可定向极小曲面，这是首个无连续对称群的完全嵌入非可定向极小曲面。证明进一步发展了等变特征值优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在S^4中构造非可定向极小曲面一直是一个开放问题，此前缺乏具有负欧拉特征数的嵌入例子。该工作旨在填补这一空白，并探索非可定向曲面在极小曲面理论中的存在性与分类问题，同时为Willmore泛函极小化提供拓扑层面的支撑。

Method: 基于作者此前发展的等变特征值优化方法（equivariant eigenvalue optimization methodology），选取精心设计的对称群族作用于非可定向曲面上，通过控制Laplacian第一规范化特征值来构造极小嵌入。同时还发现了某些自然配对的曲面与群作用不存在最大化第一规范化特征值的度量这一有趣现象。

Result: 证明了所有闭非可定向曲面均可极小嵌入S^4，首次获得负欧拉特征数的非可定向嵌入极小曲面；所有曲面面积低于8π；非可定向亏格的几何不同嵌入数至少指数增长；发现收敛到四个半球面结构的对称族，其缩放极限是R^4中首个无连续对称群的完全嵌入非可定向单周期极小曲面。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地解决了S^4中非可定向极小曲面的存在性问题，建立了面积约束下与Willmore泛函的联系，并揭示了非可定向情形下特征值优化方法的新现象（最大化解可能不存在），为后续研究提供了重要的理论基础和方法创新。

Abstract: We show that every closed, nonorientable surface can be minimally embedded in $\mathbb{S}^4$, providing in particular the first known examples of embedded, nonorientable minimal surfaces in $\mathbb{S}^4$ with negative Euler characteristic. All of the surfaces we obtain have area below $8π$, which has applications to the existence of nonorientable surfaces minimizing the Willmore functional with prescribed topology in $\mathbb{R}^n$ for $n\geq 4$. Moreover, the number of geometrically distinct embeddings of each nonorientable genus is shown to grow at least exponentially with respect to the genus.
  Among these surfaces, we identify a distinguished, highly symmetric family which converges in the large-genus limit to a union of four half-spheres, meeting along a great circle, whose poles are vertices of a regular tetrahedron. Rescalings of this family converge to a new singly-periodic nonorientable minimal surface in $\mathbb{R}^4$, which seems to provide the first example of a complete, embedded, nonorientable minimal surface in $\mathbb{R}^4$ without continuous symmetry group.
  The proofs further develop the equivariant eigenvalue optimization methodology from our earlier work, applied to carefully chosen families of symmetry groups acting on nonorientable surfaces. Interestingly, we also find natural pairs of surfaces and group actions for which there is no metric maximizing the first normalized eigenvalue of the Laplacian.

</details>


### [123] [A sharp hyperbolic volume bound for hypersurfaces in $M^3 \times \mathbb{S}^1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24057)
*Lizhi Chen,Kuntao Jin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于双曲三维流形与圆环乘积空间中切片类超曲面的尖锐体积下界定理：在标量曲率 ≥ -6 的任意光滑黎曼度量下，表示切片类 [M³×{pt}] 的闭嵌入超曲面体积不小于双曲流形 M³ 自身的体积，并完全刻画了等号成立的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有标量曲率下界约束的流形中，某些拓扑类（切片类）超曲面的极小体积问题，这是几何分析与广义相对论中类似刚性问题的自然延伸，旨在建立尖锐的几何不等式并理解其刚性特征。

Method: 该方法基于等周型论证与标量曲率的比较分析，利用切片类超曲面的同调约束，在背景流形 M³×S¹ 上结合标量曲率条件 Sc_g ≥ -6，通过变分或几何测度论技术推导超曲面的体积下界，并通过等号条件的深入分析完成刚性分类。

Result: 建立了尖锐下界：在标量曲率 ≥ -6 的度量下，表示切片类的闭嵌入超曲面体积满足 vol_g(Σ) ≥ vol_{g_hyp}(M³)。下界由乘积度量 g_hyp + h 达到；等号成立当且仅当（在保持切片类的微分同胚意义下）g = g_hyp + h 且 Σ = M³×{pt}。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了切片类超曲面体积的尖锐几何下界及完整的刚性分类，表明双曲流形的体积在该类问题中扮演着最优常数的作用，乘积度量是实现极值的唯一度量结构。

Abstract: Let $(M^3, g_{\mathrm{hyp}})$ be a closed oriented hyperbolic three-manifold normalized so that $\operatorname{sec}_{g_{\mathrm{hyp}}} \equiv -1$. We prove a sharp lower bound for the volume of hypersurfaces in $M^3 \times \mathbb{S}^1$ representing the slice class $[M^3 \times \{ \mathrm{pt} \}] \in H_3(M^3 \times \mathbb{S}^1; \mathbb{Z})$, and we classify the equality case. If $g$ is a smooth Riemannian metric on $M^3 \times \mathbb{S}^1$ with the scalar curvature $\operatorname{Sc}_g \geq -6$, then every closed embedded hypersurface $Σ$ representing the slice class $[M^3\times\{\mathrm{pt}\}]$ satisfies $\operatorname{vol}_g(Σ) \geq \operatorname{vol}_{g_{\mathrm{hyp}}}(M^3)$. The bound is attained by the product metric $g_{\mathrm{hyp}}+h$, with $h$ any metric on $\mathbb{S}^1$. Conversely, if equality holds for some $Σ$, then up to a diffeomorphism preserving the slice class, $g=g_{\mathrm{hyp}}+h$ and $Σ= M^3 \times \{\mathrm{pt}\}$.

</details>


### [124] [A counterexample to a strong maximum principle for the sixth-order GJMS operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24148)
*Liuwei Gong,Mingxiang Li,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个显式的七维黎曼流形 S²(1)×S⁵(1/100)，其Ricci曲率>0且Q曲率Q^(2)、Q^(4)、Q^(6)均为正，六阶GJMS算子P₆严格正定，但该算子不满足强极大值原理，且没有正的Green函数。该反例否证了Andrade-Piccione-Wei的猜想1及Case-Gover的一般阶推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在检验关于正Q曲率与GJMS算子正性之间关系的猜想，特别是Andrade、Piccione和Wei提出的猜想以及Case和Gover的一般阶推广。这些猜想断言在某种正曲率条件下，GJMS算子应当具有正Green函数和强极大值原理，而本文找到了一个反例来否定这些猜想。

Method: 作者选取乘积流形S²(1)×S⁵(1/100)，其中S²的截面曲率为1，S⁵的截面曲率为1/100，维度为7。通过精确计算该流形上的Ricci曲率、各阶Q曲率以及六阶GJMS算子P₆的特征值，展示P₆作为自伴算子严格正定但存在位于常数模特征值之下的非恒定正特征值，从而破坏了强极大值原理。

Result: 该例具有Ric>0、Q^(2)>0、Q^(4)>0、Q^(6)>0以及Y₂、Y₄不变量的正性，但六阶GJMS算子P₆不具备正Green函数，也满足不了强极大值原理。根本原因在于存在一个低于常数模特征值的非恒定正特征值。

Conclusion: 该反例否证了Andrade-Piccione-Wei的猜想1以及Case-Gover的一般阶猜想，表明即使在正Ricci曲率和正Q曲率的条件下，GJMS算子的正定性与强极大值原理、正Green函数的存在之间并没有必然的联系。

Abstract: We exhibit an explicit closed seven-dimensional Riemannian manifold \[ (M,g)=\mathbb S^2(1)\times \mathbb S^5\left(\frac1{100}\right), \] where the displayed parameters denote sectional curvatures, for which \(\Ric_g>0\), and hence \(Q_g^{(2)}>0\). Moreover, \[ Q^{(4)}_g>0,\qquad Q^{(6)}_g>0, \] and the sixth-order GJMS operator \(P_{6,g}\) is strictly positive as a self-adjoint operator, but nevertheless \(P_{6,g}\) fails the strong maximum principle. The failure is caused by a nonconstant positive eigenvalue of \(P_{6,g}\) lying strictly below the eigenvalue of the constant mode. The example also has \(Y_2(M,[g])>0\) and \(Y_4(M,[g])>0\), while \(P_{6,g}\) does not have a positive Green function. It disproves Conjecture~1 of Andrade, Piccione, and Wei and its general-order formulation by Case and Gover.

</details>


### [125] [Slicing Support Functions with Recovery Formula and Curvature Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24166)
*Yen-Chang Huang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入了凸体K的切片支撑函数$h_\nu(u,p)$，即在方向$\nu$处切片$K\cap\{x\cdot\nu=p\}$的支撑函数。作者推导了$h_\nu$关于$h_K$的下确界表示及相应的极小极大恒等式，并利用Fenchel-Moreau定理证明无需对$\partial K$作正则性假设即可从切片支撑函数恢复$h_K$和$K$。在三维情形下，建立了以球面曲率矩阵表示的柱面Monge-Ampère型行列式恒等式；当切向为主方向时，该行列式化简为主曲率半径的加权比。作者还刻画了主方向条件：对于$C^2$边界且高斯曲率正的凸体，球面坐标方向（除去极点）为主方向当且仅当该凸体（平移后）是旋转体。最后将构造推广到高余维迭代切片支撑函数，并通过Schur补导出全Hessian行列式恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在深入理解凸体的切片几何结构，建立从切片信息恢复整体凸体的理论框架。通过引入切片支撑函数这一新工具，作者希望不依赖任何边界正则性假设就能实现凸体的重构，并在三维情形下揭示几何量（如曲率）与切片支撑函数行列式之间的内在联系。此外，推广到高余维迭代构造的目的是建立更一般的理论框架。

Method: 本文主要采用以下方法：（1）定义切片支撑函数$h_\nu(u,p)$并将其与凸纤维体的构造联系起来；（2）推导$h_\nu$作为$h_K$的下确界卷积（infimal representation）表达式及相应的极小极大恒等式；（3）利用Fenchel-Moreau定理在无正则性假设条件下证明从切片支撑函数恢复凸体；（4）对严格凸且$C^1$边界的情形给出微分恢复公式；（5）在三维情形引入球面曲率矩阵，推导圆柱Monge-Ampère型行列式恒等式，并研究主方向条件的几何特征；（6）通过Schur complement将结果推广到高余维迭代切片支撑函数。

Result: 本文的主要结果包括：（1）建立了切片支撑函数$h_\nu$关于$h_K$的下确界表示与极小极大恒等式；（2）利用Fenchel-Moreau定理证明了无正则性假设下从$h_\nu$完全恢复$h_K$（从而恢复$K$），并在严格凸、$C^1$边界情形获得微分恢复公式；（3）在三维情形推导了以球面曲率矩阵表示的柱面Monge-Ampère型行列式恒等式，主方向情形下约化为主曲率半径的加权比；（4）证明了$C^2$边界、正高斯曲率凸体中球面坐标方向（非极点处）为主方向等价于凸体（平移后）为旋转体；（5）通过Schur补将构造推广至高余维迭代情形并导出全Hessian行列式恒等式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了切片支撑函数理论，给出了从切片数据恢复凸体的完整框架（无需正则性假设），并在三维情形揭示了切片支撑函数与凸体微分几何（曲率、主方向、旋转对称性）之间的深刻联系。该工作为凸几何分析提供了新的工具，为凸纤维体理论和高余维推广奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Let $K\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ be a convex body with support function $h_K$. For $ν\in\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$, $p\in\mathbb{R}$, and $u\inν^\perp$, we introduce the slicing support function $h_ν(u,p)$, defined as the support function of the slice $K\cap\{x\cdotν=p\}$ in the direction $u$. For each fixed $p$, this is precisely the support function of the corresponding translated fiber appearing in the construction of the convex fiber body of Mathis and Meroni \cite{MathisMeroni2023}. We derive an infimal representation of $h_ν$ in terms of $h_K$, together with a corresponding minimax identity. Using the Fenchel--Moreau theorem, we prove that $h_K$, and hence $K$, can be recovered from the slicing support function without any regularity assumption on $\partial K$. We also obtain a differential recovery formula when $K$ is strictly convex and $\partial K$ is of class $C^1$.
  In dimension three, we establish a cylindrical Monge--Ampére-type determinant identity expressed in terms of the spherical curvature matrix of $\partial K$. When the relevant tangent directions are principal directions, this determinant reduces to a weighted ratio of the corresponding principal radii of curvature. We further characterize this principal-direction condition by showing that, for convex bodies with $C^2$-boundary and positive Gaussian curvature, the spherical coordinate directions are principal directions away from the poles if and only if, up to translation, the body is a body of revolution. Finally, we extend the construction to higher-codimensional iterated slicing support functions and derive a full-Hessian determinant identity via the Schur complement.

</details>


### [126] [Reeb spaces of 1st derivatives of proper submersions of certain classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24270)
*Naoki Kitazawa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究一类特定函数类中，作为高度函数表示的proper submersions（真淹没映射）的一阶导数的Reeb空间。Reeb空间是通过将同一level set连通分量中的点等同起来得到的商空间，在Morse函数理论中具有重要地位。在proper情况下，Reeb空间在某种良性条件下自然呈现为0维或1维图，相关结果由Gelbukh和Saeki在2020年代给出。本文研究了一个非proper情况下的新案例，这是此前除作者少数工作外几乎无人涉足的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reeb空间是Morse函数理论中的核心概念，对理解函数的拓扑结构至关重要。在proper smooth实值函数的tame情况下，Reeb空间的结构已被Gelbukh和Saeki在2020年代充分研究。然而在非proper情形下，相关显式理论长期缺乏进展，这构成了本文的研究动机——填补非proper情形下Reeb空间理论的研究空白。

Method: 本文聚焦于一类特定函数类中，由高度函数表示的proper submersions（真淹没映射）的（正则）一阶导数。作者通过分析这些映射的level set连通分量的拓扑行为来研究其Reeb空间的结构，特别针对非proper情形下的一个新案例进行探讨，该案例在此前仅有作者本人的有限工作中被涉及。

Result: 作者对一类新的非proper情形下的Reeb空间结构进行了研究，给出了该新案例中的相关显式理论，这是继作者此前少数工作之后，在非proper情形下对Reeb空间理论的重要拓展。

Conclusion: 本文在非proper情形下开辟了一个新的Reeb空间研究案例，扩展了此前仅适用于proper smooth实值函数的Reeb空间图论描述框架，为非proper情形的进一步系统研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the (canonical) 1st derivatives of {\it proper} submersions represented as height functions and belonging to a certain class: a proper map means a map the preimage of a compact set by which is always compact. We investigate their {\it Reeb spaces}. They are the quotient spaces defined by the equivalence relations on the manifolds of the domains where we identify two points in a same connected component of a same level set of them. They have been important since the establishment of theory of Morse functions, in the 20th century, They are in certain tame situations $0$- or $1$-dimensional and graphs naturally. Related facts have been shown by Gelbukh and Saeki in the 2020s for certain proper smooth real-valued functions.
  In non-proper cases, even related explicit theory has been difficult, except some previously given case of the author. Our study is on a new related case.

</details>


### [127] [Positive Bakry-Émery Ricci Curvature on Homotopy Spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24394)
*Wen-Qi Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文否定了Wei和Wylie的猜想：在维度8k+1和8k+2（k≥1）中，存在带有正Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率和有界势函数的完备加权流形（具体为同伦球面），但该流形不承认非负Ricci曲率的黎曼度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wei和Wylie曾提出一个自然的问题：在具有非负Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率且有界势函数的完备加权流形上，是否一定存在非负Ricci曲率的黎曼度量？该问题涉及加权几何与经典黎曼几何之间的深层联系，对理解广义Ricci曲率下流形的几何与拓扑约束具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用了如下两步证明策略：（1）构造性结果：证明在维度至少为7的情况下，每个光滑同伦球面都承认一个加权核心度量(g,e^{-f})，满足Ric_g+Hess_g f>0（即正Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率）。（2）阻碍性结果：在维度8k+1和8k+2（k≥1）中，利用α-不变量（一个与自旋几何和指标定理相关的拓扑不变量）证明具有非零α-不变量的同伦球面不承认任何非负Ricci曲率的黎曼度量。由于这些维数中确实存在非零α-不变量的同伦球面，且紧致性保证势函数有界，从而构造出所需反例。

Result: 对于Wei–Wylie问题给出了否定答案。具体地，在每一个维度8k+1和8k+2（k≥1）中，构造了具有Ric_g+Hess_g f>0且势函数f有界的完备加权流形，但这些流形（作为具有非零α-不变量的同伦球面）不承认任何非负Ricci曲率的黎曼度量。

Conclusion: 这一结果表明，在正Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率加有界势的条件下，加权流形在拓扑上比经典非负Ricci曲率流形更为宽松，从而揭示了加权几何与经典黎曼几何之间的本质差异。同时这也展示了α-不变量作为阻碍非负Ricci曲率度量的强大工具价值。

Abstract: Wei and Wylie asked whether a complete weighted manifold with nonnegative Bakry--Émery Ricci curvature and bounded potential must admit a Riemannian metric with nonnegative Ricci curvature. We answer this question negatively in every dimension \(8k+1\) and \(8k+2\), where \(k\geq1\). Our main geometric result is that every smooth homotopy sphere of dimension at least seven admits a weighted core metric \((g,e^{-f})\) with \(\Ric_g+\Hess_g f>0\). On the other hand, in dimensions \(8k+1\) and \(8k+2\), where \(k\geq1\), we prove that a homotopy sphere with nonzero \(α\)-invariant admits no Riemannian metric with \(\Ric\geq0\). Since homotopy spheres with nonzero \(α\)-invariant exist in every dimension \(8k+1\) and \(8k+2\), and since compactness makes the potential bounded, these manifolds provide the required counterexamples.

</details>


### [128] [Uniqueness for the Kähler-Yang-Mills equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24532)
*Vamsi Pritham Pingali,Chengjian Yao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了Kähler-Yang-Mills (KYM)方程组的α-K能量泛函的公式，并利用该公式及Chen在Kähler势空间上的ε-测地线方程，证明了在具有离散自同构群的Kähler流形上，若简单向量丛的KYM方程存在解，则解唯一且α-K能量下有界。受常标量曲率Kähler度量研究的启发，作者引入了一个耦合J方程来研究α-K能量泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kähler-Yang-Mills方程组的解的唯一性和α-K能量泛函的有界性，并探索与常标量曲率Kähler度量类似的理论框架。

Method: 推导α-K能量泛函的公式，利用Chen的ε-测地线方程，结合离散自同构群假设分析KYM方程解的唯一性，并引入耦合J方程研究α-K能量泛函。

Result: 证明了在具有离散自同构群的Kähler流形上，简单向量丛KYM方程的解若存在则唯一，且α-K能量泛函下有界。

Conclusion: KYM方程解的唯一性和α-K能量的下有界性在离散自同构群条件下成立，耦合J方程为进一步研究α-K能量提供了新工具。

Abstract: A formula for the $α$-K-energy functional for the Kähler-Yang-Mills (KYM) equations is provided in this paper. Using this formula and Chen's $ε$-geodesic equation on the space of Kähler potentials, we prove that if a solution exists to the KYM equations for a simple vector bundle on a Kähler manifold with discrete automorphism group, then it is unique and the $α$-K-energy is bounded from below. Inspired by the study of constant scalar curvature Kähler metrics, we introduce a coupled J-equation to study the $α$-K energy functional.

</details>


### [129] [On the affine parametrization of null geodesics in low regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24609)
*Saúl Burgos,Leonardo García-Heveling,Melanie Graf*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文讨论低正则时空和Lorentzian长度空间中无引力曲线（类空因果曲线）的参数化问题，指出了通过类时测地线极限定义仿射参数化的方法存在非唯一性缺陷，使得极限零测地线的完备性无法良好定义，并因此探索了基于类时测地线的Penrose型奇点定理的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低正则时空和Lorentzian长度空间中，无引力曲线（achronal causal curves）扮演着零（预）测地线的角色。由于缺乏测地线方程，这些曲线没有规范参数化方式。作者希望建立一种仿射参数化概念，以便在低正则框架中延续经典奇点定理（如Penrose定理）的研究。

Method: 作者提出通过仿射参数化类时测地线的极限来定义无引力曲线的仿射参数化。特别地，他们构造了一个反例：在零超曲面两侧不连续粘接两个Lorentzian度量，所得结构仍为良好定义的Lorentzian长度空间，但该逼近过程给出非唯一的极限结果，导致极限零测地线的完备性/非完备性无法良好定义。

Result: 证明了上述仿射参数化逼近方法存在根本性缺陷：存在一个Lorentzian度量在零超曲面上不连续粘接的Lorentzian长度空间，其中不同的逼近序列导致不同的极限参数化结果，使得零测地线的完备性概念变得无意义。

Conclusion: 鉴于参数化问题本身本质上是Lorentzian特有的且无法简单克服，作者转而探索利用类时测地线而非零测地线来建立Penrose型奇点定理的可能性，从而规避零曲线参数化不确定性所带来的困难。

Abstract: In low-regularity spacetimes and Lorentzian length spaces, achronal causal curves play the role of null (pre-)geodesics. Because of the lack of a geodesic equation, they do not come with a canonical parametrization. In this context, we discuss a notion of affine parametrization via limits of affinely parametrized timelike geodesics. However, we point out a major drawback: an example where this approximation procedures gives a non-unique result, in a way that even completeness or incompleteness of the limit null geodesic is not well-defined. This example involves discontinuous gluing of two Lorentzian metrics across a null hypersurface to give a well-behaved Lorentzian length space and as such is, just like the parametrization problem itself, manifestly Lorentzian. In view of this, we explore possibilities for a Penrose-type singularity theorem using timelike geodesics.

</details>


### [130] [Structure theorems for Lichnerowicz-sharp graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24754)
*Yanlong Ding,Shiping Liu,Chiyu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Bakry-Émery曲率下界为K的有限连通简单无权重图，证明了Lichnerowicz-sharp图在移除特殊边集后具有典范纤维丛结构，其纤维与超立方体谱等谱但未必是同构的；若度数沿纤维恒定则为超立方体丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超立方体图是离散比较几何中正曲率的基本模型空间。理解Lichnerowicz-sharp图（即第一个非零Laplacian特征值恰好等于曲率下界K的图）的结构，有助于深入刻画离散正曲率空间与超立方体之间的关联。

Method: 选取所有K-特征函数均为常数的规范边集合并将其移除，然后对剩余图建立典范纤维丛结构。分析纤维的正则性、与超立方体的相似性及Laplacian谱同构性；进一步研究基图的性质（满足CD(K,∞)且第一特征值严格大于K），并利用度数沿纤维恒定条件推导纤维为超立方体的结论，最后对d≥4构造非超立方体纤维的实例。

Result: 证明了Lichnerowicz-sharp图移除规范边集后具有典范纤维丛结构：纤维正则、与超立方体结构相似且Laplacian共谱（但未必是超立方体）；基图若非平凡则满足CD(K,∞)且第一特征值严格大于K。当顶点度数沿每条典范纤维恒定时，每个纤维都是超立方体且G为超立方体丛。对每个d≥4构造了具有非超立方体典范纤维（度为d）的Lichnerowicz-sharp图。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Lichnerowicz-sharp图与超立方体之间深刻的结构联系：在移除K-特征函数恒定的边集后，图具有纤维丛结构，纤维与超立方体谱等价但可一般化；在额外正则性条件下可完全刻画为超立方体丛，同时展示了d≥4时存在非超立方体纤维的丰富例证。

Abstract: Hypercube graphs are fundamental model spaces of positive curvature in discrete comparison geometry. Let $G$ be a finite, connected, simple, unweighted graph with Bakry--Émery curvature bounded below by $K$. We call $G$ Lichnerowicz-sharp if its first non-zero non-normalized Laplacian eigenvalue $λ_1=K$. We prove that, after removing a canonical collection of edges on which every $K$-eigenfunction is constant, the resulting graph has a canonical bundle structure. Its fibers are regular, have similar structure with hypercubes, and are Laplacian-cospectral with hypercubes, although they need not themselves be hypercubes. If the base graph is nontrivial, then it satisfies $\mathrm{CD}(K,\infty)$ and has first eigenvalue strictly greater than $K$.
  As a consequence, if the vertex degree in $G$ is constant along each canonical fiber, then every fiber is a hypercube and $G$ is a hypercube bundle. Conversely, for every $d\geq 4$, we construct Lichnerowicz-sharp graphs with non-hypercube canonical fibers of degree $d$.

</details>


### [131] [Spectral Geroch conjecture and noncompact area enlargeable summands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24853)
*Daoqiang Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了：一个可能非紧的、面积可放大流形与任意自旋流形的连通和，不允许存在具有一致正标量曲率的完备黎曼度量，并将Wang-Zhang定理从闭流形推广到非紧放大流形。同时证明了广义Geroch猜想的γ-谱常数版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Wang-Zhang关于闭面积可放大流形与自旋流形连通和不存在一致正标量曲率度量的定理推广到非紧放大流形的连通和情形，并建立广义Geroch猜想的谱类比。

Method: 基于覆盖连通和构造（covering connected sum construction），结合标量-余腰（scalar-cowaist）不等式与谱-余腰（spectral-cowaist）不等式进行证明。均匀正性假设在非紧情形下以本质方式进入论证。

Result: (1) 推广了Wang-Zhang定理：非紧面积可放大流形与任意同维自旋流形的连通和不允许一致正标量曲率的完备度量；(2) 建立了γ-谱常数版本的广义Geroch猜想：当γ>(dim M1-1)/(4dim M1)时，此类连通和不存在具有正γ-谱常数的完备度量。

Conclusion: 通过覆盖连通和构造与标量-余腰、谱-余腰不等式的结合，成功将正标量曲率相关的拓扑障碍从闭流形推广到非紧流形的连通和情形，并给出了对应的谱常数版本结果。

Abstract: We prove that the connected sum of a possibly noncompact area enlargeable manifold $M_1$ with an arbitrary spin manifold $M_2$ of the same dimension admits no complete Riemannian metric of uniformly positive scalar curvature. This extends a theorem of Wang--Zhang, where $M_1$ is assumed closed, to noncompact enlargeable summands; in this generality the uniform positivity hypothesis enters the argument in an essential way. We also prove a spectral analogue of the generalized Geroch conjecture in terms of the $γ$-spectral constant: for $γ>(\dim M_1-1)/(4\dim M_1)$, such a connected sum carries no complete metric with positive $γ$-spectral constant. The proofs are based on a covering connected sum construction together with the scalar-cowaist and spectral-cowaist inequalities.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [132] [Joint moments of characteristic polynomials in the circular Jacobi ensemble and Painlevé equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24423)
*Thomas Bothner,Fei Wei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Circular Jacobi系综中特征多项式及其导数的联合矩与非线性Painlevé方程之间的联系。有限N时，联合矩由σ-Painlevé V方程的解表征；在大N极限下，极限联合矩由σ-Painlevé III'方程的解表示。作为应用，回答了Assiotis等人提出的公开问题，并将Hua-Pickrell测度遍历分解相关结果从实参数推广到复参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接随机矩阵理论中特征多项式联合矩与Painlevé方程是近年来的重要研究方向。本文旨在将这一联系从Circular Unitary系综推广到更一般的Circular Jacobi系综，并解决Assiotis等人遗留的公开问题（关于特征函数与σ-Painlevé III'方程的联系），同时将已有结果从实参数推广到复参数领域。

Method: 通过分析Circular Jacobi系综中特征多项式及其导数的联合矩，利用正交多项式与Toeplitz/Hankel行列式理论，将联合矩与σ形式Painlevé方程的解建立显式联系。对有限N情形，证明联合矩由σ-Painlevé V方程表征；在适当的N→∞标度极限下，证明极限联合矩满足σ-Painlevé III'方程。此外，将方法推广到含高阶导数的联合矩情形。

Result: (1) 对所有在容许范围内的实矩指数，有限N联合矩由σ-Painlevé V方程的解刻画；(2) 在大N标度极限下，极限联合矩由σ-Painlevé III'方程表示（对一定范围的矩指数）；(3) 回答了Assiotis等人的公开问题，证明了一随机变量的特征函数与σ-Painlevé III'方程（复参数）相关；(4) 将Hua-Pickrell测度遍历分解中出现的随机变量序列的联合矩结果从实参数推广到复参数。

Conclusion: 本文系统地建立了Circular Jacobi系综中特征多项式联合矩与Painlevé方程理论的桥梁，不仅推广了先前在Circular Unitary系综中的结果，还解决了两个重要的拓展问题：一是回答了Assiotis等人的公开问题，二是将关键结果从实参数推广至复参数，推动了随机矩阵理论与Painlevé可积系统交叉领域的发展。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a connection between joint moments of characteristic polynomials and their derivatives in the Circular Jacobi Ensemble, a generalisation of the Circular Unitary Ensemble, and solutions of nonlinear Painlevé equations. For finite $N$, we show that these joint moments are characterised by a solution of the $σ$-Painlevé V equation for all real moment exponents in their admissible range. Under an appropriate large-$N$ scaling limit, we further prove that the limiting joint moments admit a representation in terms of a solution of the $σ$-Painlevé III$'$ equation for a certain range of moment exponents. As applications, we answer a question posed by Assiotis et al. in [Math. Physics. Anal. Geom. 25 (2022), no.2, Paper No. 15, 24pp, Remark 1.7] by showing that the characteristic function of a distinguished random variable is connected with the $σ$-Painlevé III$'$ equation for complex parameters. Furthermore, we investigate joint moments involving higher-order derivatives of characteristic polynomials in the Circular Jacobi Ensemble. As a consequence, we extend a result of Assiotis et al. in [Comm. Pure Appl. Math. 79 (2026), no. 7, 1771-1827, Theorem 1.11] concerning the joint moments of a sequence of random variables arising from the ergodic decomposition of Hua-Pickrell measures, from real parameters to complex parameters.

</details>


### [133] [A Comment on Local Hypercube Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23667)
*Keyou Zhuo,Tian-Shun Chen,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了高维配分电荷函数构造中局部不等式的维度无关解析证明，取代了先前工作中低维穷举和高维数值测试的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作仅通过低维度的穷举计算建立了这些局部不等式，并在选定的更高维度中进行了数值测试，缺少统一的解析证明，难以保证在所有维度下都成立。

Method: 采用维度无关的解析方法，分别对奇数和偶数维构造中的局部不等式给出统一的证明，无需依赖计算机穷举或数值验证。

Result: 成功给出了奇数维和偶数维情况下局部不等式的解析证明，维度独立的证明替代了先前的计算验证。

Conclusion: 该工作弥补了先前只能在低维穷举验证、高维数值测试的不足，为高维配分电荷函数的不等式提供了严格的解析证明基础。

Abstract: This comment gives dimension-independent analytic proofs of the local inequalities arising in the odd- and even-dimensional constructions of higher-dimensional partition charge functions. The prior works established these inequalities by exhaustive computation in low dimensions and tested them numerically in selected higher dimensions.

</details>


### [134] [Hartree--Fock coercivity for twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23884)
*Kevin D. Stubbs,Maciej Zworski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了手性扭转双层石墨烯平带模型中Hartree-Fock泛函的极小值问题，证明了在半填充时存在恰好两个极小值（对应占据两个子带之一），并首次证明了这两个极小值处Hessian的严格强制性（coercivity），其源于组成带之间Chern数的差异，可视为"谱隙"的软版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的离散模型研究（Becker-Lin-Stubbs）证明了在半填充下Hartree-Fock泛函的极小值恰好有两个，本文希望在连续模型（Tarnopolsky-Kruchkov-Vishwanath手性模型）中获得直接类比结果；更重要的是，此前工作未涉及极小值处Hessian的严格强制性，而这对于理解能量泛函在极小值附近的局部几何性质（如同软谱隙）至关重要。

Method: 本文将先前针对离散模型的定理推广到连续版手性扭转双层石墨烯模型，在半填充条件下分析Hartree-Fock泛函的极小值结构；进一步通过对Hartree-Fock泛函在极小值处取Hessian，利用组成带的Chern数差异建立严格强制性（coercivity），即Hessian正定性的定量下界。

Result: 在半填充（多重度为2的平带半填充）时，Hartree-Fock泛函恰好有两个极小值，分别对应占据两个子带之一，这是Becker-Lin-Stubbs离散模型结果的连续版类比（该结果的拓扑推广已在Tao-Zworski讲义附录A.8中给出）。新的贡献是证明了Hessian在两个极小值处的严格强制性，其来源是组成带Chern数的差异，可被视为"谱隙"的软版本。

Conclusion: 本文建立了连续手性扭转双层石墨烯模型中Hartree-Fock问题的极小值唯一性（两个对称极小值），并通过Chern数差异导出了极小值处Hessian的严格强制性，为理解平带系统的自发对称性与能隙性质提供了分析基础。

Abstract: We study the minimisers of the Hartree--Fock functional for flat bands in the chiral model of twisted bilayer graphene introduced by Tarnopolsky, Kruchkov, and Vishwanath. Our model is a continuous version of the discrete model considered by Becker, Lin and Stubbs in previous works and the first result is a direct analogue of main theorems of those papers: at half filling of the flat band of multiplicity two, there are exactly two minimisers, obtained by occupying either one of the two sub-bands. (A more general version of this result, stressing its topological origins, was presented in Appendix A.8 of the Lecture Notes by Tao-Zworski.) The new contribution is the strict coercivity of the Hessian of the Hartree--Fock functional at the two minimisers. It is a consequence of the difference between the Chern numbers of the component bands and can be optimistically considered as a soft version of a ``spectral gap''.

</details>


### [135] [On the Dirichlet-to-Neumann conformal invariant of bounded planar domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23899)
*Alexey Kokotov,Dmitrii Korikov,Marina Nenasheva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种新的规范代表——截断光锥图（LC-diagram），用于表示C中任意连通性有界域的共形类，作为一种具有测地边界的平坦锥面。通过在该图上的Dirichlet边界问题行列式的变分公式，结合Schottky加倍和Burghelea-Friedlander-Kappeler公式，显式计算了Dirichlet-to-Neumann共形不变量detΛ/|Γ|，得到的结果仅依赖于域的Schottky加倍的周期，与Guillarmou和Guillopé通过Ruelle和Selberg zeta函数表达的公式形成互补，并与Wentworth的渐近结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典论文中GDH（Mandelstam-Giddings-Wolpert）图用于解析容量的计算，但只适用于单连通或简单连通域。本文旨在将其推广到任意连通性的有界域，并显式计算Dirichlet-to-Neumann（DN）共形不变量，该不变量此前仅有基于Ruelle和Selberg zeta函数的隐式表达（Guillarmou和Guillopé），缺乏显式初等公式。

Method: (1) 将GDH图推广为截断光锥图（LC-diagram），即具有测地边界的平坦锥面，作为任意连通性有界域共形类的规范代表；(2) 在LC-diagram上引入自然坐标并推导Dirichlet边界问题行列式关于这些坐标的变分公式；(3) 通过Schottky加倍将问题转化为紧Riemann面上的Laplacian行列式计算，利用Burghelea-Friedlander-Kappeler公式以及全纯微分模空间上的已知变分公式，消去中间项；(4) 结合Wentworth的渐近结果固定积分常数，最终得到仅用Schottky加倍周期的显式公式。

Result: 得到了DN共形不变量detΛ/|Γ|的显式初等闭式公式，该公式仅依赖于域的Schottky加倍的周期（期间矩阵），其形式远比Guillarmou-Guillopé基于zeta函数的公式简单，且与Wentworth在边界分量收缩极限下的渐近结果精确吻合，从而确定了积分常数。

Conclusion: 给出了任意连通性多连通域上DN共形不变量的显式初等公式，建立了该不变量与Schottky加倍周期之间的直接联系，为后续研究提供了比zeta函数方法更简洁的分析工具，并验证了与已有渐近结果的相容性。

Abstract: Using an analogue of Mandelstam-Giddings-Wolpert diagrams, we introduce a new canonical representative of the conformal class of a bounded domain of arbitrary connectivity in $\mathbb{C}$ as a flat conical surface with geodesic boundary (a "truncated light-cone diagram", simply LC-diagram in the sequel). The space of these diagrams can be provided with natural coordinates. We derive variational formulas for the determinant of the Dirichlet boundary problem on a LC-diagram with respect to these coordinates. Then passing to the Schottky double of the LC-diagram, making use of the Burghelea-Friedlander-Kappeler formula and the known variational formulas for determinants of Laplacians on the moduli space of holomorphic differentials lead to an explicit computation of the Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DN) conformal invariant $\frac{{\rm det}Λ}{|Γ|}$ (here $Λ$ is the DN operator on the boundary, $Γ$, of a multi-connected domain and $|Γ|$ is the length of the boundary). The resulting formula (which uses the periods of the Schottky double of the domain only) presents a conspicuously elementary counterpart to the formulas of Guillarmou and Guillopé who had expressed the DN invariant through the Ruelle and Selberg zeta-functions. Our formula agrees with the recent result of Wentworth on the asymptotics of the DN invariant as all the boundary components except one shrink, we have used this result to fix the undetermined constant of integration in our formula.

</details>


### [136] [Non-equilibrium modelling of polyatomic gases: generic framework and 11-moment model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24100)
*Masrakain Ahmad,Anil Kumar,Anirudh Singh Rana*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将GENERIC框架与11矩描述相结合，建立了多原子气体的热力学一致建模框架，统一了理性不可逆热力学(RIT)和扩展不可逆热力学(EIT)，通过显式构造Poisson矩阵和摩擦矩阵保证热力学第二定律的满足，并通过氮气激波结构计算验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多原子气体的非平衡行为需要热力学一致的理论框架来描述。现有的RIT和EIT各有侧重但缺乏统一结构，需要建立一个能够同时满足热力学第二定律、具有明确物理意义且便于实际应用的非平衡热力学模型。

Method: 将GENERIC（非平衡可逆-不可逆耦合广义方程）框架与11矩描述（包含平移温度、内温度和温度张量）相结合，通过显式构造Poisson矩阵和摩擦矩阵来统一RIT和EIT，使所有闭合关系自然地从热力学原理中涌现，并确保熵产生固有非负。

Result: 该框架保证了熵产生非负性，闭合关系由热力学原理自然导出。通过计算氮气中激波结构，与DSMC模拟及实验数据对比，在中低马赫数范围内验证了模型的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: 该GENERIC兼容的统一框架为多原子气体的非平衡输运建模提供了热力学一致的基础，在激波结构等实际应用中展现出良好的预测能力，为后续扩展到更高马赫数及其他非平衡场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this study, we present a thermodynamically consistent framework for modelling polyatomic gases by integrating the \textit{general equation for non-equilibrium reversible-irreversible coupling} (GENERIC) with an 11-moment description. Our formulation unifies \textit{rational irreversible thermodynamics} (RIT) and \textit{extended irreversible thermodynamics} (EIT) into a single GENERIC-compatible structure that guarantees compliance with the second law of thermodynamics through the explicit construction of Poisson and friction matrices. By including translational and internal temperatures, as well as a temperature tensor, the model offers a well-structured and physically meaningful representation of non-equilibrium behavior in polyatomic gases. The entropy production is inherently non-negative, and all closure relations emerge naturally from thermodynamic principles. As a test of applicability, we compute the shock wave structure in nitrogen gas, validating the framework against Direct Simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) simulations and experiments up to moderate Mach numbers.

</details>


### [137] [Boundary Free Energies, Quenched Mixing, and Gibbs-State Selection in Disordered Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24261)
*Mauris Chueng,Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最近邻无序Ising模型中的边界自由能和半空间响应，证明了固定深度切向压力的存在性及其导数与极限边界响应的关系，并给出了Gibbs态选择独立性、DLR唯一性、Gaussian法向局域化等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序系统中边界自由能的热力学极限及其与半空间响应的关系，是统计力学中核心问题。作者旨在严格刻画边界响应的存在性、Gibbs态的唯一性以及表面自由能的极限行为，同时澄清哪些一般的表面性质声称在无序情形下可能不成立。

Method: 作者采用严格数学分析方法：1）通过构造固定深度切向压力并分析其导数，在几乎处处意义下识别极限边界响应；2）在淬火指数边界响应混合条件下，证明Gibbs态选择独立性与有限深度压力的指数收敛；3）利用均匀单自旋混合条件推导DLR唯一性和Gaussian法向局域化；4）验证混合条件在(2d-1)E tanh(β|J|)<1时成立；5）在有界Dobrushin区域内建立全表面极限，并构造反例界定一般表面与刚度性声称的适用范围。

Result: 证明了固定深度切向压力存在且其导数几乎处处等于极限边界响应；在指数混合条件下获得Gibbs态选择独立性和指数收敛；建立了DLR唯一性与Gaussian法向局域化结论；确定了混合条件成立的充分条件；在有界Dobrushin区域建立了全表面极限，同时给出反例表明一般无序情形下某些表面与刚度性主张不成立。

Conclusion: 本文为无序Ising模型的边界自由能和半空间响应提供了严格的数学框架，既给出了正向的存在性、唯一性和收敛性定理，又通过反例界定了结果的适用范围，为该方向后续研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We study boundary free energies and half-space responses in nearest-neighbor disordered Ising models. First, we prove the existence of fixed-depth tangential pressure and identify its derivative almost everywhere with the limiting boundary response. Second, under quenched exponential boundary-response mixing, we prove Gibbs-state selection independence and exponential convergence of finite-depth pressures. We further show that uniform one-spin mixing yields DLR uniqueness and Gaussian normal localization, and verify the required mixing conditions whenever $(2d-1)\mathbb E\tanh(β\vert{}J\vert{})<1$. Finally, we establish an all-face surface limit in the bounded Dobrushin regime and provide counterexamples that delimit general surface and stiffness claims.

</details>


### [138] [Bound State Analysis of Rank-One Delta Interactions Supported by Deformed Hyperspheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24508)
*Hilal Demirdöğen,Fatih Erman,Merve Nur Sayar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究n维超球面上由小法向变形支持的rank-one delta相互作用。发现该相互作用的第一阶束缚态能量只依赖于平均法向位移，即变形超球面在O(ε²)阶内等效于一个半径为R-ε⟨h⟩的圆形超球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超球面（hyperspherical shell）问题上delta势的几何微扰效应，特别是理解小变形如何影响量子束缚态能量，为高维量子力学中的几何依赖问题提供理论依据。

Method: 利用归一化表面测度定义delta相互作用，将问题限制在旋转不变截面（sector）中。对未变形超球面，用修正Bessel函数积I_{ν}K_{ν}推导束缚态方程；对向内法向变形X_ε(ω)=(R-εh(ω))ω，进行ε的一阶摄动分析。

Result: 主要结果表明，在ε的一阶范围内，束缚态能量仅依赖于平均法向位移⟨h⟩。等价地，变形后的超球面在忽略O(ε²)项后，谱上等效于半径为R-ε⟨h⟩的圆形超球面。特别地，均值为零的变形在一阶不改变rank-one束缚态能量。

Conclusion: 超球面小变形对delta相互作用束缚态能量的影响在一阶完全由平均位移决定，表明该问题在最低阶具有几何平均效应的普适性；仅研究零均值扰动或不改变能量到一阶。

Abstract: We study rank-one delta interactions supported by small normal deformations of an \(n\)-dimensional hypersphere \(S_R^n\subset\mathbb{R}^{n+1}\). The interaction is defined by the normalized surface measure on the support and therefore corresponds to the rotationally invariant sector of the hyperspherical shell problem. For the undeformed hypersphere, we derive the bound-state equation for \(E=-ν^2\) in terms of the modified Bessel product \[
  I_{\frac{n-1}{2}}(νR)K_{\frac{n-1}{2}}(νR). \] We then consider an inward normal deformation \[
  X_\varepsilon(ω)=(R-\varepsilon h(ω))ω,
  \qquad ω\in S^n,
  \qquad 0<\varepsilon\ll1. \] Our main result is that, to first order in \(\varepsilon\), the bound-state energy depends only on the average normal displacement \[
  \langle h\rangle
  =
  \frac1{|S^n|}\int_{S^n}h(ω)\,dΩ_n(ω). \] Equivalently, the deformed hypersphere is spectrally equivalent, up to \(O(\varepsilon^2)\), to a round hypersphere with effective radius \(R-\varepsilon\langle h\rangle\). In particular, mean-zero deformations do not change the rank-one bound-state energy at first order.

</details>
